| 2026-11-18 18:00 UTC | 2026-11-18 20:00 UTC | Basketball - Euroleague: Besiktas - Real Madrid | The Euroleague is a competition that began its journey into the world of European basketball under the name European Champions Cup way back in 1958, and the first trophy was won by the ASK Riga team from the then USSR, i.e. today's Latvia. FIBA had the main say in this competition until 2000 when the clubs took control of the competition through their association Eurolegaue Basketball. The format of the competition has changed throughout history and included a group stage, followed by eliminations in the playoffs. The winner of this competition is decided at the final tournament called the Final Four. The most successful clubs in this competition are Real Madrid, CSKA Moscow, Maccabi Tel-Aviv, Panathinaikos, Varese, Olympiakos. |

| 2026-11-18 20:45 UTC | 2026-11-18 22:30 UTC | Handball: EHF Champions League: Wisla - Veszprem | The Handball League of Champions has its beginnings since 1955, when the competition was founded under the name of the Champions Cup. We got our first winner in the 1956-1957 season. year, and that was the team from Prague, because in that season only the city's selections performed. Today that club is called Dukla Prague. Until 1993, the competition was held under that name and was played according to the knockout cup system. Since 1993, in addition to changing the name to the Champions League, a group stage of the competition followed by eliminations has been introduced. The group stage has changed over the years, and the Final Four was introduced for the four best handball teams in Europe, after which the champion is awarded. |
